Ehtiyot bo'lingNon-linearity, collinearity, outlier, overfitting → regularization (6-bob).

Keyingi bob: 5-bob — Normal Equation vs Gradient Descent (linear modelni o'rgatishning ikki yo'li, alohida ajratib, keyin bir xil data da taqqoslanadi).

5. Normal Equation vs Gradient Descent

4-bobda linear modelni ikki yo'l bilan o'rgatish mumkin dedik. Ular shu qadar ko'p chalkashtiriladi (va intervyularda shu qadar ko'p so'raladi) ki, alohida bob ga loyiq. Bu yerda har birini alohida ajratamiz, keyin bir xil data da yuzma-yuz qo'yamiz — shunda ular aynan bir xil chiziqqa yetishini ko'rasiz.

Bitta chiziqqa ikki yo'l

"Training" nima ekanini eslang: MSE cost ni iloji boricha kichik qiladigan weight w va bias b ni topish. Buni qilishning ikkita butunlay har xil strategiyasi bor:

Yo'lG'oyaTuri
Normal EquationJavobni algebra bilan to'g'ridan-to'g'ri, bir qadamda yechishAnalitik / closed-form
Gradient DescentJavobni qadamba-qadam, pastga yurib qidirishIterativ / numerik
🧩 O'xshatish

Vodiyning eng past nuqtasini topmoqchisiz. Normal Equation = sizda to'liq xarita va formula bor, shuning uchun eng past nuqtaning aniq koordinatalarini bir zumda hisoblaysiz. Gradient Descent = tumanda turibsiz va qiyalikni his qilib, bir vaqtda bir qadam pastga yurib, yetib borasiz. Bir xil tub — yetib borishning ikki yo'li.

Normal Equation (alohida tushuntirish)

Normal Equation — eng yaxshi weight larni bitta hisobda topadigan to'g'ridan-to'g'ri formula. Loop yo'q, learning rate yo'q, "epoch" yo'q:

Normal Equationθ = (XᵀX)⁻¹ Xᵀy

U qayerdan keladi: MSE cost — convex kosa, va kosaning tubi — qiyaligi (hosilasi) aynan nol bo'lgan yagona joy. Shuning uchun cost hosilasini yozasiz, uni nolga tenglashtirasiz va parametrlarni chiziqli algebra bilan yechasiz. O'sha yechim aynan yuqoridagi formula — iteratsiya qiladigan narsa yo'q.

U aslida nima qiladi, qadamba-qadam:

  1. X ga 1 lardan iborat ustun qo'shish (shunda bias b ham weight oladi).
  2. XᵀX ni hisoblash (kichik (n+1)×(n+1) matritsa).
  3. Uni invert qilib, Xᵀy ga ko'paytirish — barcha weight lar bir vaqtda chiqadi.
import numpy as np

# X shape (m, n). bias had uchun 1 lardan ustun qo'shamiz.
X_b = np.c_[np.ones((len(X), 1)), X]

# Normal Equation, aynan o'zi:
theta = np.linalg.inv(X_b.T @ X_b) @ X_b.T @ y
# theta[0] = bias b, theta[1:] = weight lar w
✅ Afzalliklari❌ Kamchiliklari
Bir qadamda aniq javobXᵀX ni invert qilish kerak → feature lar soni bo'yicha taxminan O(n³)
Hyperparameter yo'q (sozlanadigan learning rate yo'q)Feature lar ko'p bo'lganda juda sekin (masalan, 100,000)
Iteratsiya yo'q, to'liq deterministikBarcha data bir vaqtda xotirada bo'lishi kerak
Feature scaling kerak emasXᵀX invert bo'lmasa buziladi (ortiqcha feature lar)
ℹ️ scikit-learn ning LinearRegression i shuni ishlatadi

LinearRegression().fit() ni chaqirganingizda, scikit-learn uni Normal Equation bilan yechadi (texnik jihatdan SVD pseudo-inverse, u invert bo'lmaydigan holatni ham eplaydi). Demak, LinearRegression gradient descent ISHLATMAYDI. Bu — haqiqiy intervyu "gotcha" si.

Gradient Descent (alohida tushuntirish)

Gradient Descent formulani tashlab, o'rniga qidiradi. U taxmindan boshlab, cost bo'yicha qayta-qayta pastga qadam tashlaydi — aynan 3-bobdagi dvigatel, bu yerda linear modelga qo'llanilgan:

Takrorlanadigan yangilashw := w − α · (∂cost/∂w) b := b − α · (∂cost/∂b)
# Xuddi shu linear model uchun Gradient Descent
w, b, lr, m = 0.0, 0.0, 0.01, len(X)
for epoch in range(1000):
    y_pred = w * X + b
    error  = y_pred - y
    w -= lr * (2/m) * np.sum(error * X)   # w ni pastga sur
    b -= lr * (2/m) * np.sum(error)       # b ni pastga sur
✅ Afzalliklari❌ Kamchiliklari
Juda ko'p feature ga kengayadiLearning rate tanlash kerak (tuning)
Ulkan / streaming data ga kengayadi (SGD), xotiraga sig'masa hamYetib borish uchun ko'p iteratsiya kerak
Closed-form yechimi yo'q modellar uchun ishlaydi (Logistic Regression, neural net)Yaxshi yetib borish uchun feature scaling kerak
Kam xotira (mini-batch / SGD)Faqat taqribiy — minimum yaqinida to'xtaydi
ℹ️ scikit-learn ning SGDRegressor i shuni ishlatadi

SGDRegressor linear modelni (stokastik) gradient descent bilan o'rgatadi. Xuddi shu g'oya neural network va gradient boosting ichidagi dvigatel — shuning uchun gradient descent linear regression dan ancha kengroq ahamiyatga ega.

Yuzma-yuz taqqoslash

JihatNormal EquationGradient Descent
TuriClosed-form formulaIterativ qidiruv
Learning rate?Yo'qHa (sozlash kerak)
Iteratsiya?Yo'q (bir qadam)Ha (ko'p epoch)
Feature scaling kerakmi?Yo'qHa (qattiq tavsiya etiladi)
Ko'p feature (katta n)Sekin (~O(n³))Tez — yaxshi kengayadi
Ko'p sample (katta m)Xotiraga sig'sa OKAjoyib (SGD data ni stream qiladi)
NatijaAniq optimumJuda yaqin taqribiy
Boshqa modellar uchun ishlaydimi?Yo'q (faqat linear)Ha (logistic, NN, boosting...)
scikit-learn classLinearRegressionSGDRegressor

Ishlangan misol — ikkala usul, bir xil data

Ular mos kelishini isbotlaylik. Kichik dataset: o'qigan soat → imtihon bali, haqiqiy qoida ball = 47 + 5 × soat.

import numpy as np
X = np.array([1, 2, 3, 4, 5], dtype=float)        # o'qilgan soat
y = np.array([52, 57, 62, 67, 72], dtype=float)   # imtihon bali (= 47 + 5x)

# ---- Usul 1: Normal Equation ----
X_b = np.c_[np.ones((5, 1)), X]
theta = np.linalg.inv(X_b.T @ X_b) @ X_b.T @ y
print("Normal Equation -> b =", round(theta[0], 2), " w =", round(theta[1], 2))
# Normal Equation -> b = 47.0  w = 5.0   (aniq)

# ---- Usul 2: Gradient Descent ----
w, b, lr, m = 0.0, 0.0, 0.01, len(X)
for _ in range(20000):
    err = (w * X + b) - y
    w -= lr * (2/m) * np.sum(err * X)
    b -= lr * (2/m) * np.sum(err)
print("Gradient Descent -> b =", round(b, 2), " w =", round(w, 2))
# Gradient Descent -> b = 47.0  w = 5.0   (xuddi shu chiziqqa yetdi)

Va xuddi shu ikkalasi scikit-learn orqali — har biri ichida bittadan usul ishlatadi:

from sklearn.linear_model import LinearRegression, SGDRegressor
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler

Xc = X.reshape(-1, 1)

lin = LinearRegression().fit(Xc, y)          # ichida Normal Equation
print(lin.intercept_, lin.coef_)             # ~47.0, ~5.0

# SGD ga scale qilingan feature kerak (bu GD talabi!)
Xs = StandardScaler().fit_transform(Xc)
sgd = SGDRegressor(max_iter=10000, learning_rate="invscaling").fit(Xs, y)  # ichida Gradient Descent
💡 O'quvchilarga aytiladigan asosiy fikr

Uchalasi ham bir xil chiziqqa tushadi (b≈47, w≈5). Bu omad emas — MSE cost convex (bitta kosa) bo'lgani uchun, har bir to'g'ri usul o'sha yagona global minimum ga yetadi. Normal Equation u yerga sakraydi; Gradient Descent u yerga yuradi. Yana e'tibor bering: SGD avval StandardScaler talab qildi, LinearRegression esa yo'q — bu "GD ga scaling kerak, Normal Equation ga kerak emas" ning aniq isboti.

Qaysi birini + intervyu Q&A

Tanlov yo'riqnomasi:

  • Kam feature (masalan < 10,000) va data xotiraga sig'sa → Normal Equation (LinearRegression). Aniq, sozlashsiz.
  • Ko'p feature, ulkan data yoki streaming data → Gradient Descent (SGDRegressor).
  • Closed-form yechimi yo'q har qanday model (Logistic Regression, neural net, boosting) → Gradient Descent yagona variant.
Intervyu savoliKuchli javob
Normal Equation va Gradient Descent farqi?NE weight larni formula bilan to'g'ridan-to'g'ri yechadi (closed-form, bir qadam). GD ularni cost bo'yicha pastga qadam tashlab iterativ topadi. Convex masalada natija bir xil.
Normal Equation ga learning rate kerakmi?Yo'q. GD ga kerak — bu qadam kattaligi.
Normal Equation ga feature scaling kerakmi?Yo'q. GD ga, yaxshi yetib borish uchun kerak.
Normal Equation qachon amaliy bo'lmay qoladi?Feature lar juda ko'p bo'lganda — XᵀX ni invert qilish ~O(n³) — yoki XᵀX invert bo'lmaganda (ortiqcha feature lar).
Normal Equation aniq bo'lsa, nega umuman Gradient Descent?Chunki u ulkan/streaming data ga kengayadi va closed-form yechimi yo'q modellar uchun ishlaydi (logistic regression, neural net).
scikit-learn ning LinearRegression i gradient descent ishlatadimi?Yo'q — u Normal Equation (SVD pseudo-inverse) ishlatadi. SGDRegressor — gradient descent li.
Ular har xil weight beradimi?Yo'q — convex cost da ikkalasi bir xil optimum ga yetadi (GD juda kichik numerik xatolik bilan).

Xulosa

MavzuEslab qolish kerak bo'lgan yagona narsa
Bir xil maqsadIkkalasi ham MSE ni minimallashtiradigan w, b ni topadi — faqat har xil yo'l bilan.
Normal EquationBir qadamli formula θ = (XᵀX)⁻¹Xᵀy. Aniq, sozlashsiz, lekin ~O(n³). → LinearRegression.
Gradient DescentIterativ pastga qadamlar. Learning rate + scaling kerak, lekin kengayadi va har qanday model uchun ishlaydi. → SGDRegressor.
Bir xil javobConvex cost da ikkalasi bir xil global minimum ga yetadi.
TanlovKam feature → Normal Equation. Katta/streaming data yoki non-linear model → Gradient Descent.

Keyingi bob: 6-bob — Logistic Regression (xuddi o'sha chiziqli g'oya, lekin toifa bashorat qilishga moslangan — sigmoid va "0 va 1" nihoyat to'liq yoritiladi).

6. Logistic Regression

Bu — 4-bobda boshlangan "0 va 1" hikoyasini yakunlaydigan bob. Logistic Regression — classification uchun asosiy ishchi algoritm — va chalkash nomiga qaramay, u son emas, toifa bashorat qiladi. Bu Linear Regression dan keyingi tabiiy qadam, chunki u aynan o'sha chiziqli yadrodan foydalanadi va ustiga bitta funksiya qo'shadi: sigmoid.

Logistic Regression nima?

Logistic Regression ha/yo'q savollariga javob beradi — biror narsa class ga tegishli bo'lish ehtimolini bashorat qilib, keyin o'sha ehtimolni 0 yoki 1 ga aylantiradi.

Real savolClass 1 / Class 0
Bu email spam mi?1 = spam, 0 = spam emas
Bu bemorda kasallik bormi?1 = kasallik, 0 = sog'lom
Bu mijoz ketadimi (churn)?1 = ketadi, 0 = qoladi
⚠️ Nom — tuzoq

"Logistic Regression" — bu classification algoritmi. "Regression" so'zi tarixiy (u linear regression matematikasini oladi). Agar interviewer "logistic regression classification mi yoki regression?" deb so'rasa — javob: classification.

Sigmoid funksiyasi (0–1 hikoyasi, to'liq)

Hammasi bitta funksiyaga bog'liq — sigmoid (logistic function ham deyiladi). U istalgan haqiqiy sonni (0, 1) oralig'iga siqadi:

Sigmoidσ(z) = 1 / (1 + e⁻ᶻ)

Bu — S shaklidagi egri chiziq. Katta musbat kirish → 1 ga yaqin; katta manfiy → 0 ga yaqin; aynan 0 → aynan 0.5:

z−5−2025
σ(z)0.0070.120.50.880.993

Bu chiqish probability sifatida o'qiladi. σ(z) = 0.88 — "88% class 1 bo'lishi mumkin" degani. Siz so'ragan "0 va 1" ning manbai aynan shu: bu linear regression emas — bu sigmoid 0 va 1 orasidagi probability ishlab chiqarmoqda.

Linear dan logistic ga (to'liq pipeline)

Logistic Regression — bu aynan Linear Regression chiqishini sigmoid dan o'tkazish. Uch qadam:

To'liq bashorat pipeline i1) z = b + w₁x₁ + ... + wₙxₙ (chiziqli qism — istalgan son) 2) p = σ(z) = 1/(1+e⁻ᶻ) (probability 0..1 ga siqish) 3) class = 1 agar p ≥ 0.5 bo'lsa, aks holda 0 (yakuniy 0/1 ga threshold)

Ishlangan misol (Brownlee dan): bo'ydan jinsni (male/female) bashorat qilish, o'rganilgan b = −100, w = 0.6, bo'y 150 sm uchun:

  • z = −100 + 0.6 × 150 = −10
  • p = σ(−10) ≈ 0.0000454 → "male" class i ehtimoli taxminan 0%
  • p < 0.5 → class 0 (female) bashorat qilinadi
ℹ️ Nega bu 4-bobga nisbatan muhim

4-bobda "Linear Regression 0 va 1 chiqarMAYDI" dedik. Endi nima chiqarishini aniq ko'rasiz: o'sha b + w·x, sigmoid ga o'ralib, keyin threshold qilinadi. Linear Regression 1-qadamda to'xtaydi; Logistic Regression 2 va 3-qadamlarni qo'shadi.

💡 Decision boundary

Kesim nuqtasi p = 0.5 bo'lgani uchun, va σ(z) = 0.5 aynan z = 0 bo'lganda, modelning decision boundary si b + w·x = 0 chizig'i. Demak Logistic Regression class lar orasiga to'g'ri chiziqli chegara chizadi — u linear classifier.

Log-odds va koeffitsiyentlar nimani anglatadi

Tenglamani qayta tartibga solsangiz, chiziqli qism class 1 ning log-odds iga (logit ham deyiladi) teng bo'ladi:

Log-odds (logit)ln( p / (1 − p) ) = b + w⋅x

"Odds" = hodisa ehtimoli ÷ hodisa bo'lmaslik ehtimoli (masalan, p=0.8 → odds 4). Bu koeffitsiyentlarga toza talqin beradi — intervyu sevimli nuqtasi:

ℹ️ Logistic koeffitsiyentni talqin qilish

Feature bir birlikka oshsa log-odds w ga o'zgaradi, ya'ni odds eḴ ga ko'payadi. Misol: "kunlik sigaret" uchun w = 0.7 bo'lsa, har bir qo'shimcha sigaret kasallik odds ini e⁰·⁷ ≈ 2.0 ga ko'paytiradi — ikki barobar qiladi. (Eslatma: logistic da koeffitsiyent odds ga ta'sir qiladi, to'g'ridan-to'g'ri probability ga emas.)

Log loss (va nega MSE emas)

Logistic Regression MSE emas, log loss (binary cross-entropy) ni minimallashtirishga o'rgatiladi:

Log loss (bitta sample)loss = −[ y·ln(p) + (1 − y)·ln(1 − p) ]

Intuitiv o'qing: agar haqiqiy label 1 bo'lsa, loss −ln(p) — p 1 ga yaqin bo'lsa juda kichik, lekin p 0 ga ketsa cheksizlikka portlaydi. Ya'ni u ishonch bilan qilingan xato javoblarni shafqatsiz jazolaydi — probability modelidan aynan shu kerak.

⚠️ Nega shunchaki MSE emas?

Ikki sabab. (1) Ichida sigmoid bilan MSE non-convex bo'ladi — gradient descent tiqilib qoladigan local minimum larga to'la. Log loss convex qoladi. (2) MSE ishonch bilan qilingan xato bashoratlarni deyarli jazolamaydi. Log loss — probability uchun to'g'ri vosita.

O'rganish + scikit-learn da

Logistic regression uchun closed-form / Normal Equation YO'Q — shuning uchun u gradient descent bilan o'rgatiladi (texnik jihatdan likelihood ni maksimallashtirish = log loss ni minimallashtirish). Aynan shuning uchun 5-bob muhim: bu yerda gradient descent yagona yo'l.

from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression

# max_iter yetib borishga yordam beradi; avval feature larni scale qiling
model = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000)
model.fit(X_train, y_train)

y_pred  = model.predict(X_test)            # yakuniy 0/1 label lar
y_proba = model.predict_proba(X_test)[:, 1]  # class 1 ehtimoli
print(model.coef_, model.intercept_)        # w lar va b (log-odds)
💡 predict vs predict_proba + threshold tuning

predict default 0.5 kesimini ishlatadi. Lekin threshold ni siljitish mumkin: kasallik aniqlashda ko'proq holatni ushlash uchun class 1 ni p ≥ 0.3 da belgilashingiz mumkin (yuqori recall). Shuning uchun predict_proba juda foydali — u o'zingiz threshold qilishingiz uchun probability beradi (14-bob).

Multiclass (softmax / one-vs-rest)

Sigmoid ikki class ni eplaydi. 3+ class uchun ikki strategiya bor, scikit-learn birini avtomatik tanlaydi:

  • Softmax (multinomial) — sigmoid ni umumlashtiradi, har bir class uchun probability beradi, hammasi 1 ga yig'iladi. Bitta model, zamonaviy default.
  • One-vs-Rest (OvR) — har bir class uchun bitta binary classifier o'rgatadi ("bu A class mi yoki yo'q?") va eng yuqorisini tanlaydi. Oddiy, eskiroq yondashuv.

Qachon ishlatish + gotcha lar

✅ Ajoyib❌ Zaif
Tez, tushunarli baseline classifier kerak bo'lsaHaqiqiy chegara juda non-linear bo'lsa
Faqat label emas, kalibrlangan probability kerak bo'lsaMurakkab feature o'zaro ta'siri muhim bo'lsa (tree lar yaxshiroq)
Class lar taxminan chiziqli ajraladigan bo'lsaKo'p ahamiyatsiz / kuchli korrelyatsiyalangan feature bo'lsa
  • Murakkablik: training tez; prediction shunchaki dot product + sigmoid (juda tez).
  • Muhim hyperparameter lar: C (regularization kuchining teskarisi — kichik C = ko'proq regularization), penalty (l2/l1), solver, class_weight (imbalance uchun), max_iter.
  • Ko'p uchraydigan xatolar (notebook laringizdan): feature larni scale qilishni unutish (u gradient/regularization ga asoslangan) → sekin yetib borish yoki "failed to converge" ogohlantirishi; scaling yoki kattaroq max_iter bilan tuzating. Imbalanced data da accuracy ga ishonmang — class_weight="balanced" va Precision/Recall ishlating (14-bob).

Intervyu savollari

SavolKuchli javob
Logistic regression classification mi yoki regression?Classification — nomidagi "regression" tarixiy.
Linear regression dan farqi?Bir xil chiziqli yadro b+w·x, lekin probability uchun sigmoid dan o'tkaziladi, keyin class ga threshold qilinadi.
Sigmoid nima qiladi?Istalgan haqiqiy sonni (0,1) ga siqadi, shunda u probability sifatida o'qiladi.
Cost function nima? Nega MSE emas?Log loss (cross-entropy). MSE+sigmoid non-convex va ishonchli xatolarni kam jazolaydi.
Closed-form yechimi bormi?Yo'q — u gradient descent (maximum likelihood) bilan o'rgatiladi.
Koeffitsiyentlar nimani anglatadi?Feature ning har birligi log-odds ni w ga o'zgartiradi, ya'ni odds ni e^w ga ko'paytiradi.
Decision boundary nima?z = b+w·x = 0 (p = 0.5) chizig'i — chiziqli chegara.
3+ class ni qanday eplaysiz?Softmax (multinomial) yoki One-vs-Rest.
Class imbalance ni qanday eplaysiz?class_weight="balanced", threshold ni siljitish, va accuracy emas Precision/Recall bilan baholash.
Nega yetib bormay (converge) qolishi mumkin?Scale qilinmagan yoki kuchli korrelyatsiyalangan feature lar — data ni scale qiling yoki max_iter ni oshiring.

Xulosa

MavzuEslab qolish kerak bo'lgan yagona narsa
NimaChiziqli classifier — probability bashorat qiladi, keyin class ga threshold qiladi.
Sigmoidσ(z)=1/(1+e⁻ᶻ) istalgan sonni 0–1 probability ga siqadi. "0 va 1" shu yerdan keladi.
Pipelinez = b+w·x → sigmoid → probability → threshold 0.5 → class.
CostLog loss (cross-entropy), MSE emas. Convex, ishonchli xatolarni jazolaydi.
TrainingClosed-form yo'q → gradient descent (max likelihood).
KoeffitsiyentlarLog-odds ga ta'sir qiladi; e^w — odds ko'paytiruvchisi.
sklearnLogisticRegression; predict_proba ishlating, feature larni scale qiling, C regularization ni boshqaradi.

Keyingi bob: 7-bob — Regularization (Ridge, Lasso, Elastic Net — linear va logistic modellarda overfitting ni boshqaradigan C/α).

7. Regularization

4 va 6-boblar bir xil ogohlantirish bilan yakunlandi: ko'p feature bilan (ayniqsa korrelyatsiyalangan), linear va logistic modellar overfit qiladi. Regularization — bu davo. Bu yangi algoritm emas — bu cost function ga kichik o'zgartirish bo'lib, modellarni sodda saqlaydi, va u ML da hamma joyda uchraydi (linear modellar, logistic regression, SVM, neural net).

Nega regularize qilamiz?

Overfit qiladigan modelda odatda katta, beqaror weight lar bo'ladi — u har bir training nuqtasini quvib qattiq egiladi. Regularization buni cost ga katta weight lar uchun jarima qo'shib yengadi. Endi model ikki maqsadni muvozanatlashi kerak: data ga moslash va weight larni kichik saqlash.

Asosiy g'oyaYangi cost = (moslik xatosi, masalan MSE) + α × (weight kattaligiga jarima)
🧩 O'xshatish

Bu — biror narsani iloji boricha kam so'z bilan tushuntirishi kerak bo'lgan o'quvchiga o'xshaydi. Qisqalikka majburlash uni haddan tashqari murakkablashtirish va yodlashdan to'xtatadi — u sodda, umumiy haqiqatga undaladi. Regularization modelni "kichik so'zlar" (kichik weight lar) ishlatishga majbur qiladi.

L1 vs L2 (asosiy g'oya — eng ko'p so'raladigan juftlik)

"Weight lar qanchalik katta" ni o'lchashning ikki yo'li bor, va tanlov modelning xulqini butunlay o'zgartiradi. Bu L1 vs L2 farqi — eng ko'p beriladigan intervyu savollaridan biri.

L2 (Ridge)L1 (Lasso)
Jarimakvadrat weight lar yig'indisi, Σw²absolyut weight lar yig'indisi, Σ|w|
Weight larga ta'siriHamma weight ni silliq 0 ga kichraytiradiBa'zi weight larni aynan 0 ga suradi
Feature selection?Yo'q — hamma feature ni saqlaydi (kichik)Ha — 0 bo'lgan feature lar tashlanadi
NatijaDense (hamma feature ozgina ishlatiladi)Sparse (faqat foydali feature lar qoladi)
ℹ️ Nega L1 nolga aylantiradi, L2 esa yo'q?

Geometrik intuitsiya: L1 ning chegara sohasi — romb (o'qlarda o'tkir burchaklar), shuning uchun eng yaxshi yechim ko'pincha aynan burchakka tushadi — u yerda ba'zi weight lar 0. L2 ning sohasi — doira (burchaksiz), shuning uchun u weight larni kichraytiradi, lekin kamdan-kam aynan 0 ga tegadi. Shuning uchun Lasso avtomatik feature selection vazifasini ham bajaradi.

Ridge / Lasso / Elastic Net

Uchta regularize qilingan linear model — bu shunchaki "MSE + jarima":

Uchta cost functionRidge : J = MSE + α · Σ w² (L2) Lasso : J = MSE + α · Σ |w| (L1) ElasticNet : J = MSE + r·α·Σ|w| + (1−r)·α·Σ w² (ikkalasi aralash)
ModelJarimaNima uchun mos
RidgeL2Yaxshi default; ko'p feature hammasi bir oz foydali; korrelyatsiyalangan feature lar
LassoL1Faqat bir nechta feature muhim deb gumon qilsangiz (avtomatik selection kerak)
Elastic NetL1 + L2Ko'p feature, ba'zilari korrelyatsiyalangan — ikkalasidan eng yaxshisi; feature > sample bo'lganda Lasso dan xavfsizroq
💡 Real misol

Uy narxini 100 feature dan bashorat qilish, ko'pchiligi ustma-ust (total_rooms, bedrooms, sqft...). Lasso ortiqcha lar weight ini 0 ga qo'yadi va narxga aslida ta'sir qiladigan qisqa, tushunarli ro'yxatni qoldiradi — bepul feature-selection hisoboti.

Kuch tugmasi (alpha / C) — klassik gotcha

Bitta hyperparameter qancha regularization qo'llashni boshqaradi. Lekin u har xil class da boshqacha nomlanadi — va teskari yo'nalishda:

ClassTugmaYo'nalish
Ridge, Lasso, ElasticNetalphaKatta alpha = KO'PROQ regularization (soddaroq model)
LogisticRegression, SVMCKatta C = KAMROQ regularization (C = 1/alpha)
⚠️ Ikki gotcha

(1) alpha va C teskari yo'nalishda — ularni chalkashtirish juda ko'p uchraydigan intervyu/amaliyot xatosi. (2) Regularize qilishdan oldin doim feature larni scale qiling. Jarima weight kattaligiga asoslangan, shuning uchun feature lar har xil scale da bo'lsa, jarima nohaqlik bilan qo'llanadi. Avval StandardScaler ishlating (ideal holda Pipeline ichida).

scikit-learn da

from sklearn.linear_model import Ridge, Lasso, ElasticNet

ridge = Ridge(alpha=1.0).fit(X_train, y_train)        # L2
lasso = Lasso(alpha=0.1).fit(X_train, y_train)        # L1 - ba'zi coef lar 0 bo'ladi
enet  = ElasticNet(alpha=0.1, l1_ratio=0.5).fit(X_train, y_train)  # aralash (r = l1_ratio)

print((lasso.coef_ == 0).sum(), "ta feature Lasso tomonidan tashlandi")

# Logistic Regression default da L2-regularized; C — (teskari) tugma
from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
clf = LogisticRegression(C=0.5, penalty="l2", max_iter=1000)

Qaysi birini (yo'riqnoma)

  • Ridge dan boshlang — Géron maslahati: "deyarli doim hech bo'lmaganda ozgina regularization bo'lsin", va Ridge — xavfsiz default.
  • Lasso ishlating agar faqat bir nechta feature muhim deb hisoblasangiz va ularni avtomatik tanlashni xohlasangiz.
  • Elastic Net ishlating ko'p feature, ba'zilari korrelyatsiyalangan bo'lsa — u odatda Lasso dan afzal, chunki Lasso feature > sample yoki kuchli korrelyatsiya bo'lganda beqaror harakat qilishi mumkin.
  • Bonus — Early stopping: iterativ modellar uchun validation xatosi ko'tarila boshlaganda training ni to'xtating. Geoffrey Hinton uni "go'zal bepul tushlik" deb atagan. Bu — cost function ga tegmasdan regularization.

Intervyu savollari

SavolKuchli javob
Regularization nima?Cost ga katta weight lar uchun jarima qo'shish — modelni sodda saqlab overfitting ni kamaytirish.
L1 vs L2?L1 (Lasso) Σ|w| ni jazolaydi va ba'zi weight larni aynan 0 ga suradi (sparse, feature selection); L2 (Ridge) Σw² ni jazolaydi va hamma weight ni silliq kichraytiradi (dense).
Nega L1 sparsity beradi?Uning romb shaklidagi chegarasi o'qlarda burchaklarga ega, shuning uchun optimum ko'pincha ba'zi weight lar aynan 0 bo'lgan joyga tushadi.
Elastic Net nima?L1 va L2 aralashmasi (nisbat l1_ratio); ko'p, ehtimol korrelyatsiyalangan feature bo'lganda yaxshi.
alpha nimani boshqaradi? C-chi?alpha = regularization kuchi (katta = ko'proq). C = LogisticRegression/SVM da 1/alpha (katta C = kamroq regularization).
Avval feature larni scale qilish kerakmi?Ha — jarima weight kattaligiga bog'liq, shuning uchun feature lar bir xil scale da bo'lishi kerak.
Bias had regularize qilinadimi?Yo'q — faqat feature weight lari jazolanadi, intercept emas.
Lasso interpretability ga qanday yordam beradi?Foydasiz feature larni 0 ga aylantirib, aslida muhim bo'lganlarning qisqa ro'yxatini qoldiradi.

Xulosa

MavzuEslab qolish kerak bo'lgan yagona narsa
NimaCost ga katta weight lar uchun jarima qo'shish → soddaroq model → kamroq overfitting.
L2 / RidgeΣw²; hamma weight ni kichraytiradi; yaxshi default.
L1 / LassoΣ|w|; weight larni 0 ga aylantiradi → avtomatik feature selection.
Elastic NetIkkalasi aralash; ko'p/korrelyatsiyalangan feature lar uchun eng yaxshi.
Kuchalpha ↑ = ko'proq regularization; C ↑ = kamroq. Doim avval scale qiling.

Keyingi bob: 8-bob — K-Nearest Neighbors (birinchi non-linear, "dangasa" algoritmimiz — training yo'q, faqat yodlab, ovoz beradi).

8. K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN)

To'liq boshqacha yondashuv. Linear va logistic regression weight larni o'rgandi; KNN hech narsa o'rganmaydi. U shunchaki training data ni yodlab oladi va bashorat so'ralganda, eng yaqin tanish misollarga qarab ularni nusxalaydi. Bu — ML dagi eng intuitiv algoritm — va masofa, hyperparameter ning bias-variance roli va curse of dimensionality ni tanishtirishning ajoyib yo'li.

KNN nima? ("dangasa" o'rganuvchi)

Yangi nuqtani classify qilish uchun KNN so'raydi: "Mening K ta eng yaqin qo'shnim kim, va ular nima?" — keyin ko'pchilik ovozini oladi.

🧩 O'xshatish

"Do'stlaringni ayt — kimligingni aytaman." Yangi odam haqida taxmin qilish uchun unga eng o'xshash bir nechta odamga qarab, ular o'xshash deb faraz qilamiz. Mahalla uy narxini bashorat qilish uchun eng yaqin bir nechta uyga qaraymiz.

KNN uchta mashhur laqabga ega, har biri haqiqiy xususiyatni tasvirlaydi — hammasi keng tarqalgan intervyu atamalari:

NomChunki...
Lazy learner (dangasa)Training paytida hech ish qilmaydi — hamma ish bashoratda bo'ladi.
Instance-basedUning "model" i — aynan saqlangan training instance lar; o'rganilgan parameter yo'q.
NonparametricData shakli haqida hech qanday taxmin qilmaydi (1-bob).
ℹ️ G'alati narx profili

"Training" bir zumda (shunchaki data ni saqlash, O(1)). Lekin prediction sekin — bitta nuqtani classify qilish uchun u har bir training nuqtasiga masofani o'lchashi kerak (O(n)). Bu ko'p modellarning aksi (sekin train, tez predict). Ajoyib intervyu qarama-qarshiligi.

Qanday bashorat qiladi

  1. Yangi nuqtadan har bir training nuqtasigacha masofa ni o'lchang.
  2. Eng yaqin K tasini tanlang ("qo'shnilar").
  3. Classification: qo'shnilar class larining ko'pchilik ovozi. Regression: qo'shnilar qiymatlarining o'rtachasi (yoki mediana).

U hatto probability ham beradi: K=5 va 4 ta qo'shni class 1 bo'lsa, P(class 1) = 4/5 = 0.8.

Masofa metrikalari

"Eng yaqin" masofa ta'rifini talab qiladi. Uchta keng tarqalgan metod bor — va hammasi bitta umumiy formulaning (Minkowski) xususiy holatlari:

3 ta masofa metodiEuclidean (L2): d = √( Σᵢ (aᵢ − bᵢ)² ) to'g'ri chiziqli masofa Manhattan (L1): d = Σᵢ |aᵢ − bᵢ| absolyut farqlar yig'indisi ("shahar bloklari") Minkowski (p): d = ( Σᵢ |aᵢ − bᵢ|ᵖ )^(1/p) umumiy shakl → p = 2 → Euclidean, p = 1 → Manhattan
MetrikaG'oyaQachon
Euclidean (L2)To'g'ri chiziqli masofaFeature lar bir xil turdagi (default)
Manhattan (L1)Absolyut farqlar yig'indisi ("shahar bloklari")Feature lar har xil turdagi (age, height, ...)
MinkowskiIkkalasining umumlashmasi (p parametri)sklearn ning default oilasi (p=2 = Euclidean)

K ni tanlash (bu bias-variance tugmasi)

K — yagona hyperparameter, va u 1-bobdagi bias-variance kelishuvini to'g'ridan-to'g'ri boshqaradi:

KXulqiXavf
Kichik (K=1)Chegara har bir nuqtaga yopishadi — juda moslashuvchanLow bias, high variance → overfitting (bitta shovqinli nuqta javobni o'zgartiradi)
KattaSilliq, o'rtachalangan chegaraHigh bias, low variance → underfitting (juda katta K shunchaki ko'pchilik class ni bashorat qiladi)
💡 Amaliy K maslahatlari

K ni ko'p qiymat sinab (masalan, 1–21) va validation da eng yaxshisini tanlab sozlang — aynan notebook laringizdagi "find optimal K" loop. Binary classification uchun ovoz tengligidan qochish uchun toq K ishlating. Boshlang'ich nuqta sifatida K ≈ √n keng tarqalgan qoida.

No'ldan (from scratch)

KNN shu qadar oddiy ki, uni 4 qatorda yozish mumkin — training loop yo'q, faqat masofa + ovoz:

import numpy as np

def knn_predict(X_train, y_train, x_new, k=3):
    dists = np.sqrt(((X_train - x_new) ** 2).sum(axis=1))  # har bir nuqtaga Euclidean
    nearest = dists.argsort()[:k]                          # eng yaqin k tasining indekslari
    return np.bincount(y_train[nearest]).argmax()          # ko'pchilik ovozi

scikit-learn da (avval SCALE qilish SHART)

from sklearn.neighbors import KNeighborsClassifier
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler

# KNN uchun scaling ixtiyoriy EMAS - u masofaga asoslangan
scaler = StandardScaler()
X_train_s = scaler.fit_transform(X_train)
X_test_s  = scaler.transform(X_test)

knn = KNeighborsClassifier(n_neighbors=5)   # K = 5
knn.fit(X_train_s, y_train)
print(knn.score(X_test_s, y_test))
⚠️ KNN dagi #1 xato: scale qilmaslik

KNN masofa ustiga qurilgan, shuning uchun katta diapazonli feature (salary 0–100,000) kichigini (age 0–100) butunlay bosib ketadi — model age ni amalda e'tiborsiz qoldiradi. KNN dan oldin doim StandardScaler (yoki MinMax). Part 3 notebook ingiz to'g'ri X_train_scaled da fit qiladi — bu to'g'ri odat.

Qachon ishlatish + curse of dimensionality

✅ Yaxshi❌ Zaif
Kichik/o'rta dataset, kam featureKatta dataset (sekin prediction)
Oddiy, intuitiv baseline kerak bo'lsaKo'p feature (curse of dimensionality)
Decision boundary notekis bo'lsaFeature lar scale qilinmagan yoki missing value to'la
⚠️ Curse of dimensionality

KNN kam feature bilan porlaydi, lekin ko'p feature bilan buziladi. O'lchamlar oshgani sayin makon shu qadar ulkan bo'ladiki, har bir nuqta boshqasidan uzoq bo'ladi — "eng yaqin" ma'nosini yo'qotadi va masofaga asoslangan usullar ishlamay qoladi. Yechim: feature selection yoki dimensionality reduction (PCA, 16-bob). Bu — klassik intervyu tushunchasi.

Intervyu savollari

SavolKuchli javob
Nega KNN "dangasa" o'rganuvchi deyiladi?U training qilmaydi — shunchaki data ni saqlaydi va hamma ishni bashorat paytida qiladi.
KNN parametric mi yoki nonparametric?Nonparametric — o'rganilgan parameter yo'q, data shakli haqida taxmin yo'q.
U qanday bashorat qiladi?Masofa bo'yicha K eng yaqin nuqtani topadi; classification = ko'pchilik ovozi, regression = o'rtacha.
Nega KNN uchun feature larni scale qilish kerak?U masofaga asoslangan; scale qilinmagan katta diapazonli feature lar masofani bosib ketadi.
Kichik vs katta K ta'siri?Kichik K = low bias/high variance (overfit); katta K = high bias/low variance (underfit).
Nega toq K ishlatamiz?Binary classification da ovoz tengligidan qochish uchun.
Curse of dimensionality nima?Yuqori o'lchamlarda hamma nuqta taxminan teng uzoqlikda bo'ladi, shuning uchun "eng yaqin" ma'nosini yo'qotadi va KNN buziladi.
Training vs prediction narxi?Training O(1) (faqat saqlash); prediction har so'rov uchun O(n) (hamma nuqtaga taqqoslash) — katta hajmda sekin.
Default masofa metrikasi?Euclidean (Minkowski p=2 bilan).

Xulosa

MavzuEslab qolish kerak bo'lgan yagona narsa
NimaK eng o'xshash saqlangan misolni nusxalab bashorat qiladi (ovoz / o'rtacha).
LazyTraining yo'q; hamma ish bashoratda. Nonparametric, instance-based.
MasofaDefault Euclidean; "eng yaqin" masofa metrikasini talab qiladi.
KKichik = overfit, katta = underfit. Binary uchun toq K. Sozlang.
Scale shartMasofaga asoslangan → doim avval StandardScaler/MinMax.
EhtiyotKatta data da sekin; ko'p feature bilan curse of dimensionality.

Keyingi bob: 9-bob — Naive Bayes (Bayes teoremasiga qurilgan tez, probability ga asoslangan classifier).

9. Naive Bayes

Naive Bayes — probability ga asoslangan classifier — oddiy, juda tez va kutilmaganda kuchli, ayniqsa text uchun (spam filter, sentiment). U butunlay statistikadan bitta formula (Bayes teoremasi) va bitta dadil soddalashtiruvchi taxminga qurilgan. Gradient descent yo'q, weight yo'q — faqat sanash.

Bayes teoremasi

Bayes teoremasi shartli ehtimolni teskari aylantiradi. Classification uchun u javob beradi: "shu feature lar berilganda, har bir class qanchalik ehtimolli?"

Bayes teoremasiP(class | data) = [ P(data | class) × P(class) ] / P(data)
HadNomiMa'nosi
P(class | data)PosteriorBizga kerak: feature lar berilganda class ehtimoli
P(data | class)LikelihoodClass to'g'ri bo'lsa, bu feature lar qanchalik ehtimolli
P(class)PriorClass umuman qanchalik keng tarqalgan (feature lardan oldin)
P(data)EvidenceFeature lar qanchalik keng tarqalgan (normalizatsiya konstantasi)

Classify qilish uchun har bir class uchun posterior ni hisoblab, eng yuqorisini tanlaymiz — bu MAP (maximum a posteriori) qarori. P(data) har bir class uchun bir xil bo'lgani uchun, uni tashlab, faqat P(data | class) × P(class) ni taqqoslashimiz mumkin.

"Naive" taxmin

Barcha feature lar uchun P(data | class) ni birgalikda hisoblash qiyin. Shuning uchun Naive Bayes bitta dadil yo'l tutadi: u barcha feature lar class berilganda mustaqil deb faraz qiladi. Bu har bir feature ehtimolini alohida ko'paytirishga imkon beradi:

Naive mustaqillik taxminiP(x₁, x₂, ..., xₙ | class) ≈ P(x₁|class) × P(x₂|class) × ... × P(xₙ|class)
ℹ️ Nega "naive" (sodda)?

Mustaqillik taxmini real data da deyarli har doim noto'g'ri (email da "free" va "money" so'zlari aniq bog'liq). Bu — sodda ishonch, shuning uchun bu nom. Hayratlanarlisi: algoritm amalda baribir juda yaxshi ishlaydi, chunki classification uchun bizga aniq probability emas, faqat to'g'ri class g'olib chiqishi kerak.

Qanday classify qiladi (ishlangan misol)

Training — shunchaki sanash — optimization yo'q. U data dan ikki narsani hisoblaydi:

  • Class prior lar — masalan, P(go-out) = (go-out kunlar soni) / (jami kunlar).
  • Shartli ehtimollar — masalan, P(weather=sunny | go-out) = (quyoshli go-out kunlar) / (go-out kunlar).

Misol (Brownlee dan): quyoshli kun va ishlaydigan mashina uchun go-out vs stay-home ni bashorat qilish. Har bir class uchun ko'paytiramiz:

Har class uchun ball (keyin max ni tanlash)go-out = P(sunny|go-out) × P(working|go-out) × P(go-out) stay-home = P(sunny|stay) × P(working|stay) × P(stay)

Qaysi ball kattaroq bo'lsa — bashorat o'sha; ikkalasini normalize qilib, ularni probability ga aylantiramiz. Butun algoritm shu — sanoqlar bir-biriga ko'paytiriladi.

Variantlar (feature turiga qarab tanlang)

VariantFeature lar qanday bo'lsa...Klassik qo'llanish
GaussianNBUzluksiz sonlar (har class uchun qo'ng'iroq egri chizig'i)Umumiy numeric data
MultinomialNBSanoq / chastotaText (so'z sanoqlari, TF-IDF) — Part 9 ingiz
BernoulliNBBinary (0/1, bor/yo'q)Text — so'z bor/yo'q belgilari

Variantlar orasida o'zgaradigan yagona narsa — P(feature | class) qanday hisoblanishi:

Har variant uchun P(feature | class)GaussianNB: P(x|c) = 1 / √(2πσ²) · e^( −(x−μ)² / 2σ² ) (μ, σ har feature, har class uchun) MultinomialNB: P(word|c) = ( count(word, c) + α ) / ( total_words(c) + α·V ) (V = lug'at hajmi) BernoulliNB: har feature ning bor/yo'qligini (1/0) ishlatadi, o'z smoothing i bilan

GaussianNB uchun "training" — bu shunchaki har bir class ichida har feature ning o'rtachasi (μ) va standard deviation (σ) ini hisoblash, keyin yangi qiymatlarni o'sha qo'ng'iroq egri chizig'i formulasiga qo'yish.

from sklearn.naive_bayes import GaussianNB, MultinomialNB, BernoulliNB

GaussianNB().fit(X_train, y_train)      # uzluksiz feature lar
MultinomialNB(alpha=1.0).fit(X_tr, y)   # sanoq / TF-IDF (text)
BernoulliNB().fit(X_bin, y)             # binary bor/yo'q feature lar

Zero-frequency muammosi va smoothing

⚠️ Bitta nol hammasini buzadi

Naive Bayes ehtimollarni ko'paytirgani uchun, agar biror feature qiymati training da class bilan hech uchramagan bo'lsa, uning shartli ehtimoli 0 — va 0 × har narsa = 0, butun class ballini o'chiradi. Bitta ko'rilmagan so'z bashoratni veto qiladi.

Yechim — Laplace (additive) smoothing: har bir sanoqqa kichik son (odatda 1) qo'shish, shunda hech narsa aynan nol bo'lmaydi. scikit-learn da bu alpha hyperparameter (default 1.0).

scikit-learn da (spam / text)

Bu aynan Part 9 NLP pipeline ingiz — TF-IDF vectorizer MultinomialNB ga uzatadi:

from sklearn.pipeline import Pipeline
from sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer
from sklearn.naive_bayes import MultinomialNB

text_clf = Pipeline([
    ("tfidf", TfidfVectorizer()),   # text ni so'z feature lariga aylantirish
    ("clf",   MultinomialNB()),     # default alpha=1.0 smoothing
])
text_clf.fit(X_train, y_train)
text_clf.predict(["win a free prize now"])   # -> ehtimol 'spam'

Qachon ishlatish + gotcha lar

✅ Ajoyib❌ Zaif
Text classification (spam, sentiment, mavzular)Feature lar kuchli bog'liq va bu muhim bo'lsa
Juda yuqori o'lchamli data (minglab so'z)Yaxshi kalibrlangan probability kerak bo'lsa (NB niki haddan tashqari ishonchli)
Tez baseline / kichik data / real-time kerak bo'lsaJuda non-Gaussian numeric feature lar (GaussianNB uchun)
ℹ️ Generative vs discriminative (intervyu oltini)

Naive Bayes — generative model — u data qanday yaratilishini modellashtiradi, P(data|class). Logistic Regression — discriminative — u chegarani, P(class|data) ni to'g'ridan-to'g'ri modellashtiradi. Sevimli "bu ikkalasini solishtir" intervyu savoli.

Intervyu savollari

SavolKuchli javob
Bayes teoremasini ayting.P(class|data) = P(data|class)·P(class) / P(data).
Nega "naive" deyiladi?U barcha feature larni class berilganda mustaqil deb faraz qiladi — odatda noto'g'ri, lekin baribir ishlaydi.
Noto'g'ri taxminga qaramay nega ishlaydi?Classification uchun bizga faqat to'g'ri class eng yuqori ballga ega bo'lishi kerak, aniq probability emas.
Zero-frequency muammosi nima? Yechim?Ko'rilmagan feature qiymati 0 probability beradi va ko'paytmani nolga aylantiradi. Laplace smoothing (alpha) bilan tuzatiladi.
Text uchun qaysi variant?MultinomialNB (so'z sanoqlari / TF-IDF); bor/yo'q uchun BernoulliNB.
Nega tez?Training — shunchaki chastotalarni sanash — iterativ optimization yo'q.
Generative mi yoki discriminative?Generative (P(data|class) ni modellashtiradi); discriminative Logistic Regression bilan qarama-qarshi.
Prior vs likelihood vs posterior?Prior P(class); likelihood P(data|class); posterior P(class|data) — biz bashorat qiladigan narsa.

Xulosa

MavzuEslab qolish kerak bo'lgan yagona narsa
NimaBayes teoremasi orqali probability classifier; eng yuqori posterior li class ni tanlaydi.
"Naive"Feature lar class berilganda mustaqil deb faraz qiladi — noto'g'ri, lekin samarali.
TrainingShunchaki chastota sanash → juda tez, kichik data da ishlaydi.
VariantlarGaussian (uzluksiz), Multinomial (sanoq/text), Bernoulli (binary).
SmoothingLaplace (alpha) zero-probability tuzog'idan qutqaradi.
Eng yaxshiText/spam va yuqori o'lchamli data; kuchli tez baseline.

Keyingi bob: 10-bob — Support Vector Machines ("eng keng ko'cha" classifier, margin lar va kernel trick).

10. Support Vector Machines (SVM)

SVM lar 1990-yillarning eng ko'p muhokama qilingan algoritmi edi va hozir ham kam tuning bilan kuchli natija uchun asosiy tanlov. Asosiy g'oyalar — margin, support vector lar va kernel trick — sevimli intervyu mavzulari va chinakam nafis.

SVM nima? (eng keng ko'cha)

Logistic regression kabi, SVM ikki class ni to'g'ri chegara (hyperplane) bilan ajratadi. Lekin u maxsus birini tanlaydi: class lar orasidagi eng keng bo'shliqning o'rtasida turadigan chegarani.

🧩 O'xshatish (eng keng ko'cha)

Ikki guruh nuqta orasiga, hech bir nuqta yo'lga tushmasdan, iloji boricha eng keng ko'cha qurishni tasavvur qiling. SVM ning decision boundary si — o'sha ko'chaning markaziy chizig'i. Kengroq ko'cha = xato uchun ko'proq joy = yaxshiroq generalization. (Shuning uchun SVM "large margin classifier" ham deyiladi.)

Margin va support vector lar

SVM ning yuragi bo'lgan ikki ta'rif:

  • Margin — o'sha ko'cha kengligi: chegaradan har bir class ning eng yaqin nuqtalarigacha bo'lgan masofa. SVM uni maksimallashtiradi.
  • Support vector lar — aynan ko'cha chekkasida turgan bir nechta nuqta. Faqat ular chegarani belgilaydi — boshqa istalgan nuqtani siljiting yoki o'chiring, hech narsa o'zgarmaydi; support vector ni siljiting — chegara siljiydi.
Hyperplane (2 feature)b + w₁x₁ + w₂x₂ = 0 (chiziq ustida → bitta class, ostida → boshqasi)
ℹ️ Nega "support vector" lar muhim

Faqat support vector lar modelni belgilagani uchun, SVM xotira-tejamkor (u qolgan barcha nuqtalarni unutishi mumkin) va uzoqdagi nuqtalarga chidamli. Bu — keng tarqalgan intervyu nuqtasi: "support vector lar nima va nega muhim?"

Soft margin va C parametri

Real data mukammal ajralmaydi — toza ko'cha (hard margin) talab qilish ishlamaydi yoki overfit qiladi. Shuning uchun SVM soft margin ishlatadi: u ba'zi nuqtalarga ko'cha ichida yoki noto'g'ri tomonda turishga ruxsat beradi, buni C hyperparameter boshqaradi:

CMarginTa'siri
Kichik CKengroq ko'cha, ko'proq buzilishga ruxsatKo'proq regularization → yuqori bias, past variance
Katta CTorroq ko'cha, kam buzilishKamroq regularization → past bias, yuqori variance (overfit xavfi)
ℹ️ C yo'nalishi 7-bob bilan bir xil

Logistic Regression dagi kabi, katta C = kamroq regularization. scikit-learn ning SVC va LogisticRegression da izchil.

Kernel trick (SVM ning super kuchi)

Hozircha chegara — to'g'ri chiziq. Lekin class larni chiziq bilan ajratib bo'lmasa-chi (masalan, bir class boshqasining ichida halqa hosil qilsa)? Kernel trick buni hal qiladi.

🧩 O'xshatish

Tekis stol ustida ko'k nuqtalar atrofida halqa shaklidagi qizil nuqtalarni tasavvur qiling — hech qanday to'g'ri chiziq ularni ajratmaydi. Endi ichki nuqtalarni 3D ga "ko'taring": birdan tekis varaq (plane) ular orasidan o'tishi mumkin. Kernel bu ko'tarishni yuqori o'lchamlarga bajaradi — yangi koordinatalarni hech qachon hisoblamasdan (bu — "trick": u faqat kerakli dot product larni hisoblaydi).

KernelChegara shakliAsosiy tugma
linearTo'g'ri chiziq / plane—
polyEgri (polynomial)degree
rbf (radial, default)Moslashuvchan yopiq sohalargamma
Kernel funksiyalari K(x, xᵢ)Linear: K(x, xᵢ) = x · xᵢ (oddiy dot product) Polynomial: K(x, xᵢ) = (1 + x · xᵢ)ᵈ (daraja d) RBF / radial: K(x, xᵢ) = e^( −gamma · ||x − xᵢ||² ) Bashorat: f(x) = b + Σᵢ aᵢ · K(x, xᵢ) (faqat support vector lar bo'yicha yig'indi)
💡 gamma nima qiladi (RBF)

gamma bitta training nuqtasining ta'siri qanchalik uzoqqa yetishini belgilaydi. Kichik gamma = uzoq yetish = silliq chegara (underfit mumkin). Katta gamma = qisqa yetish = nuqtalarga yopishgan egri chegara (overfit mumkin). C va gamma ni birga sozlang (15-bob).

scikit-learn da (SCALE qilish SHART)

from sklearn.svm import SVC
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from sklearn.pipeline import make_pipeline

# SVM masofa/margin ga asoslangan -> scaling shart
model = make_pipeline(
    StandardScaler(),
    SVC(kernel="rbf", C=1.0, gamma="scale")   # predict_proba kerak bo'lsa probability=True
)
model.fit(X_train, y_train)
print(model.score(X_test, y_test))
⚠️ Gotcha lar

(1) Doim feature larni scale qiling — SVM margin/masofaga asoslangan (Part 3 notebook ingiz SVC dan oldin scale qiladi). (2) SVC default da probability bermaydi — predict_proba kerak bo'lsa probability=True qo'ying (sekinroq). (3) SVC katta data da sekin (taxminan O(n²–n³)); katta data uchun LinearSVC yoki SGDClassifier ishlating. (4) Regression uchun SVR bor.

SVM turlari va variantlari (qaysi class)

"SVM" — bu oila. scikit-learn bir nechta class beradi — qaysi birini tanlashni bilish amaliy (va intervyu) ko'nikmasi:

ClassNimaNima uchun mos
SVCKernel SVM classifier (rbf/poly/linear)Kichik/o'rta data; non-linear chegara. Default tanlov.
LinearSVCFaqat linear classifier (tezroq solver)Katta / yuqori o'lchamli / sparse data (masalan text). SVC(kernel="linear") dan ancha tez.
NuSVCSVC kabi, lekin C o'rniga nuSupport vector / xatolar ulushini to'g'ridan-to'g'ri boshqarmoqchi bo'lsangiz (nu ∈ (0,1]).
SVR / LinearSVRRegression versiyalariSVM g'oyasi bilan son bashorat qilish (margin o'rniga "epsilon tube").
 
from sklearn.svm import SVC, LinearSVC, NuSVC, SVR

SVC(kernel="rbf", C=1.0, gamma="scale")   # kernel classifier (default tanlov)
LinearSVC(C=1.0)                          # tez linear classifier - katta/text data
NuSVC(nu=0.5)                             # nu = xatolar ulushining yuqori chegarasi
SVR(kernel="rbf", C=1.0, epsilon=0.1)     # regression (epsilon-tube)
💡 Tezkor qoida

Non-linear, kichik data → rbf bilan SVC. Linear, katta yoki text data → LinearSVC. Class emas, son kerak → SVR.

Qachon ishlatish + afzallik/kamchilik

✅ Yaxshi❌ Zaif
Kichik/o'rta dataset, aniq marginJuda katta dataset (sekin train)
Yuqori o'lchamli data (hatto feature > sample, masalan text)Probability yoki interpretability kerak bo'lsa
Non-linear chegara (RBF/poly kernel orqali)Ko'p shovqin / ustma-ust class lar

Intervyu savollari

SavolKuchli javob
SVM nimani optimallashtiradi?Margin ni maksimallashtiradigan hyperplane ni — class lar orasidagi eng keng bo'shliq.
Support vector lar nima?Margin chekkasidagi nuqtalar; faqat ular chegarani belgilaydi.
Hard vs soft margin?Hard = buzilishsiz (mukammal ajraladigan data kerak); soft = buzilishga ruxsat, C boshqaradi.
C nima qiladi?Margin buzilishini boshqaradi: kichik C = kengroq margin / ko'proq regularization; katta C = torroq / kamroq regularization.
Kernel trick nima?Data ni yashirin tarzda yuqori o'lchamlarga aylantirish (kernel = o'sha yerdagi dot product) — shunda chiziqli chegara non-linear data ni ajratadi, koordinatalarni hisoblamasdan.
gamma nima qiladi (RBF)?Har nuqtaning ta'sir doirasini belgilaydi: kichik = silliq (underfit), katta = egri (overfit).
SVM uchun scale qilish kerakmi?Ha — u margin/masofaga asoslangan.
Nega SVM sekin bo'lishi mumkin?Training ~O(n²–n³); juda katta data uchun yomon. LinearSVC/SGD ishlating.

Xulosa

MavzuEslab qolish kerak bo'lgan yagona narsa
NimaClass lar orasida eng keng margin li chegarani topadi.
Support vector larFaqat o'zlari chegarani belgilaydigan chekka nuqtalar.
Soft margin / CBuzilishga ruxsat; katta C = kamroq regularization.
Kernel trickYuqori o'lchamlarda chiziqli chegara → bu yerda non-linear chegara. rbf/poly/linear.
gammaRBF doirasi: kichik = silliq, katta = overfit.
Scale shartMargin ga asoslangan → avval StandardScaler. Katta data da sekin.

Keyingi bob: 11-bob — Decision Trees (CART) — tushunarli "20 savol" modeli va forest hamda boosting ning poydevori.

11. Decision Trees (CART)

CART = Classification And Regression Trees. Decision tree — ML dagi eng oson o'qiladigan model — ha/yo'q savollaridan iborat flowchart. Unga jiddiy e'tibor berishga arziydi, chunki u ikkita eng kuchli klassik algoritmning qurilish bloki: Random Forest (12-bob) va Gradient Boosting (13-bob).

Decision Tree nima?

U data ni bir qator ha/yo'q savollari bilan bo'ladi, toki ishonchli javobga (leaf) yetguncha.

🧩 O'xshatish (20 savol / Akinator)

Bu — "20 savol" o'yini: "Bo'y > 180sm mi? → yo'q. Vazn > 80kg mi? → yo'q → Female bashorat." Har bir savol qidiruvni toraytiradi, toki javob aniq bo'lguncha. Shifokorning tashxis flowchart i yoki kredit tasdiqlash ro'yxati — aynan decision tree.

Agar Height > 180 sm           -> Male
Aks holda Weight > 80 kg       -> Male
Aks holda                      -> Female

Har bir ichki node bitta feature ni tekshiradi; har bir branch — javob; har bir leaf bashoratni beradi (classification uchun class, regression uchun son).

Qanday bo'linadi (greedy recursive splitting)

Training tree ni recursive binary splitting bilan quradi: har bir node da u har bir feature va har bir mumkin bo'lgan split nuqtasini sinab, natijaviy guruhlar qanchalik "toza" ekani bo'yicha baholaydi va eng yaxshisini greedy tanlaydi. Keyin har bir child da takrorlaydi — yuqoridan pastga, bir vaqtda bitta eng yaxshi split.

"Toza" — har bir guruh asosan bitta class dan iborat degani. Child larni eng toza qiladigan split g'olib.

Gini vs Entropy (formulalar)

Classification uchun node ning impurity sini o'lchashning ikki yo'li — yana klassik intervyu juftligi:

Impurity o'lchovlari (classification)Gini impurity: G = Σₖ pₖ(1 − pₖ) 0 = toza, 0.5 = eng yomon (binary) Entropy: H = − Σₖ pₖ log₂(pₖ) 0 = toza, 1.0 = eng yomon (binary) Information Gain = H(parent) − child larning vaznli o'rtacha H si (entropy ning "qancha o'rgandik")
GiniEntropy
O'lchaydiTasodifiy tanlovni noto'g'ri belgilash ehtimoliInformation / "ajablanish"
TezlikTezroq (logarifm yo'q)Bir oz sekinroq (log)
NatijaDeyarli doim juda o'xshash tree lar

scikit-learn da criterion="gini" (default) yoki "entropy". Regression tree lar uchun Gini yo'q — split lar kvadrat xatoni (Σ(yᵢ − ŷ)²) minimallashtiradi va leaf o'z sample larining o'rtachasini bashorat qiladi.

💡 Qaysi birini tanlash?

Kamdan-kam ahamiyatga ega — ular juda o'xshash tree beradi. Gini default, chunki u bir oz tezroq. Tanlashga vaqt sarflamang; max_depth ga sarflang.

Overfitting va pruning

⚠️ Tree lar tabiatan overfit qiladi

Cheklanmasa, tree har bir leaf bitta training nuqtasi bo'lguncha bo'linaverib, data ni mukammal yodlab oladi (shovqin bilan birga). To'liq o'sgan tree — high variance ning darslik misoli: bir nechta training qatorini o'zgartiring va butunlay boshqa tree olasiz.

Ikki davo:

  • Pre-pruning (erta to'xtatish): max_depth, min_samples_split, min_samples_leaf, max_leaf_nodes.
  • Post-pruning: to'liq o'stirib, keyin zaif shoxlarni kesish — cost-complexity pruning, ccp_alpha orqali (katta alpha = ko'proq pruning).

scikit-learn da (scaling shart emas!)

from sklearn.tree import DecisionTreeClassifier

# StandardScaler kerak emas! Tree threshold bo'yicha bo'linadi, scale ahamiyatsiz.
tree = DecisionTreeClassifier(max_depth=4, random_state=42)
tree.fit(X_train, y_train)
print(tree.score(X_test, y_test))
print(tree.feature_importances_)   # split lar qaysi feature larga tayangani
ℹ️ Katta farq: scaling yo'q

KNN, SVM va Logistic Regression dan farqli, decision tree larga feature scaling kerak emas — "age > 30" kabi split birlikdan qat'i nazar bir xil ishlaydi. Bu — sevimli intervyu farqi (tree-based vs distance/gradient-based modellar).

Qachon ishlatish + gotcha lar

✅ Kuchli tomonlari❌ Zaif tomonlari
To'liq tushunarli ("white box" — qoidalarni o'qib bo'ladi)Oson overfit qiladi; high variance
Scaling yo'q; aralash numeric + categorical, non-linear ni eplaydiBoxy, o'qqa moslangan chegaralar (diagonal chiza olmaydi)
Tez; feature importance beradiBeqaror — kichik data o'zgarishi → boshqa tree
ℹ️ Nega bu bob eng muhim

Bitta tree kamdan-kam eng yaxshi model — lekin uning zaifligi (high variance) aynan ensemble lar tuzatadigan narsa. Ko'p tree ni o'rtachalash → Random Forest (12-bob). Bir-birining xatosini tuzatadigan tree lar qurish → Boosting (13-bob). Oldinda hamma narsa shunga qurilgan.

Intervyu savollari

SavolKuchli javob
CART nimani anglatadi?Classification And Regression Trees.
Tree qayerda bo'linishni qanday hal qiladi?Greedy recursive binary splitting — barcha feature/threshold ni sinab, impurity ni eng ko'p kamaytiradigan split ni tanlaydi.
Gini vs Entropy?Ikkalasi node impurity sini o'lchaydi (0 = toza). Gini tezroq (log yo'q); entropy information gain bilan bog'liq. Natijalar deyarli bir xil.
Regression tree qanday bo'linadi?Kvadrat xatoni minimallashtiradi; leaf sample larining o'rtachasini bashorat qiladi.
Nega tree overfit qiladi, qanday oldini olasiz?Leaf lar toza bo'lguncha bo'linadi (yodlaydi). max_depth / min_samples_leaf (pre-prune) yoki ccp_alpha (post-prune) bilan oldini oling.
Decision tree larga feature scaling kerakmi?Yo'q — ular threshold bo'yicha bo'linadi, scale ahamiyatsiz.
Ular high mi yoki low variance?High variance (beqaror) — shuning uchun ularni ensemble qilamiz.
Ular qanday decision boundary yasaydi?O'qqa moslangan, boxy to'rtburchaklar.

Xulosa

MavzuEslab qolish kerak bo'lgan yagona narsa
NimaHa/yo'q split lardan iborat flowchart; leaf lar bashorat beradi. To'liq tushunarli.
SplittingGreedy: impurity ni eng ko'p kamaytiradigan split ni tanlaydi.
ImpurityGini = Σpₖ(1−pₖ); Entropy = −Σpₖlog₂pₖ. Regression kvadrat xatoni minimallashtiradi.
OverfittingTree lar overfit qiladi (high variance); max_depth / pruning (ccp_alpha) bilan boshqaring.
Scaling yo'qTree-based → scaling kerak emas (KNN/SVM/LogReg dan farqli).
PoydevorRandom Forest (12-bob) va Boosting (13-bob) asosi.

Keyingi bob: 12-bob — Bagging va Random Forest (variance ni yo'qotish uchun ko'p tree ni o'rtachalash).

12. Bagging va Random Forest

11-bob muammo bilan tugadi: bitta decision tree kuchli, lekin high-variance (beqaror). Bu bob — yechim: ko'p tree ni ensemble ga birlashtirish. Random Forest — butun ML dagi eng mashhur, ishonchli algoritmlardan biri va Kaggle ishchi oti.

Ensemble lar (olomon donoligi)

Ensemble ko'p modelni bitta kuchliroq predictor ga birlashtiradi.

🧩 O'xshatish

Bitta ekspertdan so'rasangiz, noto'g'ri yoki omadsiz javob olishingiz mumkin. Mingta xilma-xil odamdan so'rab, ularning taxminlarini o'rtachalasangiz, olomon odatda hayratlanarli darajada aniq. Ko'p zaif, xilma-xil fikr bitta kuchli fikrga birlashadi — bu "olomon donoligi".

Ikkita ajoyib ensemble oilasi bor (va bu — muhim intervyu farqi):

  • Bagging — ko'p modelni har xil data da parallel o'rgatib, keyin o'rtachalash. Variance ni kamaytiradi. (Bu bob.)
  • Boosting — modellarni ketma-ket o'rgatib, har biri oldingisining xatosini tuzatadi. Bias ni kamaytiradi. (13-bob.)

Bagging (Bootstrap Aggregating)

Ikki so'z: Bootstrap + Aggregating.

  • Bootstrap = data dan ko'p tasodifiy sub-sample yasash, qaytarish bilan (bir xil qator bir necha marta uchrashi mumkin).
  • Aggregate = har bir sub-sample da bitta model o'rgatib, keyin birlashtirish: classification uchun ovoz, regression uchun o'rtacha.
Bagging 3 qadamda1) N ta bootstrap sample oling (tasodifiy, qaytarish bilan) 2) Har bir sample da bitta model (odatda chuqur tree) o'rgating 3) Bashorat = ko'pchilik ovozi (classification) yoki o'rtacha (regression)
ℹ️ Nega variance ni kamaytiradi

Har bir tree o'z sample ini boshqacha overfit qiladi, shuning uchun ularning xatolari qisman tasodifiy va mustaqil. O'rtachalash o'sha tasodifiy xatoni yo'qotadi — bitta tree ning chayqalishi silliqlanadi. Shuning uchun bagging chuqur decision tree kabi high-variance modellar uchun mukammal sherik.

Random Forest (bitta aqlli o'zgartirish)

Oddiy bagged tree larning zaifligi bor: ular greedy, shuning uchun ko'pincha birinchi split uchun bir xil kuchli feature ni tanlaydi — bu tree larni korrelyatsiyalangan qiladi, korrelyatsiyalangan modellarni o'rtachalash esa kam yordam beradi. Random Forest = tree lar bagging i + bitta o'zgartirish:

ℹ️ Forest yasaydigan o'zgartirish

Har bir split da tree faqat tasodifiy feature to'plamini ko'rib chiqishi mumkin (hammasini emas). Bu tree larni bir-biridan farqli — decorrelated — qiladi, shuning uchun ularni o'rtachalash ancha ko'proq yordam beradi. Keng tarqalgan default: classification uchun har split da m = √p feature (regression uchun p/3), bu yerda p = jami feature.

🧩 O'xshatish

Agar har bir saylovchi faqat bir xil gazetani o'qisa, hammasi bir xil ovoz beradi (korrelyatsiyalangan). Har birini har xil tasodifiy manbalar aralashmasini o'qishga majburlang — olomonning umumiy qarori ancha donoroq bo'ladi. Tasodifiy feature to'plami — o'sha "har xil manbalar".

OOB score va feature importance — ikki bepul sovg'a

  • Out-of-Bag (OOB) score — har bir bootstrap qatorlarning ~37% ini tashlab ketadi; har bir tree ko'rmagan qatorlarda sinaladi va alohida split kerak bo'lmasdan bepul validation score beradi (oob_score=True).
  • Feature importance — har bir feature barcha tree lar bo'ylab impurity ni qancha kamaytirganini o'rtachalash qaysi feature lar muhim ekanini ko'rsatadi (feature_importances_).

scikit-learn da

from sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier

rf = RandomForestClassifier(
    n_estimators=200,     # tree lar soni (ko'p = yaxshi, keyin plateau)
    max_features="sqrt",  # m = √p har split uchun feature (RF o'zgartirishi)
    max_depth=None,       # tree lar ataylab chuqur o'stiriladi
    oob_score=True,       # bepul validation score
    n_jobs=-1, random_state=42)
rf.fit(X_train, y_train)   # scaling kerak emas (tree-based)
print(rf.oob_score_, rf.feature_importances_)
💡 Ko'p tree hech qachon overfit qilmaydi

Bitta chuqur tree dan farqli, Random Forest ga ko'proq tree qo'shish overfit qilmaydi — aniqlik shunchaki plateau ga chiqadi. Shuning uchun n_estimators ni compute byudjetingiz imkon bergancha baland qo'ying. Yana: ExtraTreesClassifier yanada ko'proq randomness qo'shadi (tasodifiy split threshold lar) — tezroq training va ba'zan yaxshiroq natija.

Bagging vs Boosting (intervyu juftligi)

Bagging (Random Forest)Boosting (13-bob)
TrainingParallel va mustaqilKetma-ket (har biri oldingisini tuzatadi)
Asosan kamaytiradiVarianceBias
Base modellarChuqur (low-bias, high-variance) tree larSayoz (high-bias) tree / stump lar
OverfittingChidamli (ko'p tree xavfsiz)Overfit qilishi mumkin (ehtiyot kerak)
TezlikParallel (tez)Ketma-ket (sekinroq)

Qachon ishlatish + gotcha lar

✅ Kuchli tomonlari❌ Zaif tomonlari
Kam tuning bilan kuchli, ishonchli aniqlikBitta tree dan kam tushunarli ("forest" qoidalar)
Overfitting ga chidamli; non-linear, aralash data ni eplaydiKattaroq/sekinroq model; ko'proq xotira
Scaling yo'q; importance va OOB score beradiTabular musobaqalarda ko'pincha boosting yutadi

Intervyu savollari

SavolKuchli javob
Bagging nima?Ko'p modelni bootstrap sample larda (tasodifiy, qaytarish bilan) o'rgatib, variance ni kamaytirish uchun aggregate (ovoz/o'rtacha) qilish.
Bootstrap sample nima?Data ning qaytarish bilan olingan tasodifiy sample i (bir xil qator takrorlanishi mumkin).
Random Forest oddiy bagging dan qanday farq qiladi?U har split da tasodifiy feature to'plamini ham tanlaydi, bu tree larni decorrelate qiladi, shuning uchun o'rtachalash ko'proq yordam beradi.
Bagging vs Boosting?Bagging = parallel, variance ni kamaytiradi; Boosting = ketma-ket, bias ni kamaytiradi.
OOB score nima?Har bootstrap dan tashlab ketilgan ~37% qatordan olingan validation aniqligi — test xatosining bepul bahosi.
Ko'p tree Random Forest ni overfit qiladimi?Yo'q — aniqlik plateau ga chiqadi; ko'p tree xavfsiz (faqat sekinroq).
Default max_features?Classification uchun √p, regression uchun p/3.
RF ga feature scaling kerakmi?Yo'q — u tree-based.

Xulosa

MavzuEslab qolish kerak bo'lgan yagona narsa
EnsembleKo'p modelni birlashtirish → olomon donoligi. Bagging (variance) vs Boosting (bias).
BaggingBootstrap sample larda o'rgatib + aggregate → high-variance modellar variance ini kamaytiradi.
Random ForestBagged tree lar + har split da tasodifiy feature to'plami → decorrelated, kuchliroq.
Bepul sovg'alarOOB score (bepul validation) + feature_importances_.
AmaliyKam tuning, overfitting ga chidamli, scaling yo'q; n_estimators ni bemalol oshiring.

Keyingi bob: 13-bob — Boosting (AdaBoost, Gradient Boosting va XGBoost/LightGBM/CatBoost — Kaggle chempionlari).

13. Boosting (XGBoost / LightGBM / CatBoost)

Agar Random Forest ishonchli ishchi ot bo'lsa, boosting — chempion. Gradient-boosted tree lar tabular data musobaqalarining ko'pchiligini yutadi. Bu bob g'oyadan → AdaBoost → Gradient Boosting → Advanced ML notebook laringiz yoritadigan uchta zamonaviy kutubxona (XGBoost, LightGBM, CatBoost) gacha quriladi.

Boosting g'oyasi

Bagging (12-bob) tree larni mustaqil o'rgatib o'rtachalaydi. Boosting esa aksini qiladi: u tree larni ketma-ket o'rgatadi, har biri o'zidan oldingilarning xatolarini tuzatadi. Ko'p zaif o'rganuvchi (ko'pincha sayoz tree / "stump") bitta kuchli o'rganuvchiga birlashadi.

🧩 O'xshatish

O'quvchi mashq testini topshiradi, qaysi savollarni xato qilganini ko'radi va keyingi urinishda aynan o'shalarni qattiqroq o'rganadi — kam xato qolguncha takrorlaydi. Har bir bosqich oldingi bosqichning xatolariga e'tibor beradi. Bu — boosting.

ℹ️ Boosting bias ni kamaytiradi

Har bir model ensemble ning residual xatolarini tuzatgani uchun, boosting asosan bias ga (underfitting) hujum qiladi — bagging ning aksi, u variance ga hujum qiladi. Shuning uchun boosting sayoz, high-bias base tree lardan foydalanadi, Random Forest esa chuqurlaridan.

AdaBoost (Adaptive Boosting)

Birinchi muvaffaqiyatli boosting algoritmi. U data ni qayta vaznlaydi: joriy model xato qilgan nuqtalar kattaroq vazn oladi, shunda keyingi stump ularga e'tibor beradi.

  1. Har bir sample ni teng vazn bilan boshlang (1/n).
  2. Zaif o'rganuvchi (decision stump) o'rgating; uning vaznli xatosini hisoblang.
  3. Stump ga aniqligiga qarab ovoz (stage weight) bering.
  4. Xato classify qilingan nuqtalar vaznini oshiring; keyingi stump ni qayta vaznlangan data da o'rgating.
  5. Yakuniy bashorat = barcha stump larning vaznli ovozi.
AdaBoost stump vaznistage = ln( (1 − error) / error ) (aniqroq stump → kattaroq ovoz) vazn yangilash: w ← w × e^(stage × perror) (perror = 1 agar xato, 0 agar to'g'ri)

Gradient Boosting

Zamonaviy, umumiyroq versiya. Nuqtalarni qayta vaznlash o'rniga, har bir yangi tree residual xatolarni (ensemble hali ham xato qiladigan narsani) bashorat qilishga o'rgatiladi — bu loss ning gradient i, shuning uchun bu nom.

Gradient Boosting (loop)1) Oddiy bashoratdan boshlang (masalan, o'rtacha) 2) residuals = haqiqat − joriy bashorat ni hisoblang 3) O'sha residual larni bashorat qiladigan kichik tree o'rgating 4) Uni qo'shing (learning rate ga ko'paytirilgan): F ← F + lr × tree 5) n_estimators marta takrorlang
💡 learning_rate × n_estimators (asosiy kelishuv)

learning_rate (shrinkage) har bir tree ning hissasini masshtablaydi. Kichik learning rate (masalan, 0.05) + ko'proq tree yaxshiroq generalize qiladi, lekin sekinroq o'rgatadi. Kichik lr + ko'p tree + early stopping — g'olib retsept. (Bu — 3-bobdagi aynan o'sha gradient-descent α g'oyasi.)

XGBoost / LightGBM / CatBoost (zamonaviy uchlik)

Bularning hammasi optimallashtirilgan gradient boosting — Kaggle ni yutadigan vositalar. Farqlar amaliy:

KutubxonaAsosiy fokusNima uchun mos
XGBoostRegularize qilingan GB, level-wise tree lar, juda mustahkamIsbotlangan default; hamma joyda kuchli
LightGBMLeaf-wise o'sish + histogram binning → juda tezKatta dataset; tezlik; native categorical
CatBoostOrdered boosting + eng yaxshi native categorical ishloviKo'p categorical feature; eng kam tuning
ℹ️ Tezkor tanlov

Ko'p categorical ustun → CatBoost. Ulkan dataset / tezlik kerak → LightGBM. Xavfsiz, yaxshi hujjatlangan default → XGBoost. Uchalasi ham tabular data da odatda Random Forest ni yutadi — ko'proq tuning evaziga.

Asosiy hyperparameter lar

ParametrNima qiladi
n_estimatorsTree lar soni (boosting bosqichlari). Juda ko'p → overfit.
learning_rateHar tree uchun shrinkage. Pastroq = ko'proq tree kerak, yaxshiroq generalize.
max_depthTree chuqurligi (boosting sayoz tree ishlatadi, masalan 3–8).
subsample / colsample_bytreeQator / ustun sampling → randomness qo'shadi, overfitting ga qarshi.
reg_alpha / reg_lambdaLeaf weight lariga L1 / L2 regularization (7-bob).
early_stoppingValidation score yaxshilanmay qolganda to'xtatish — shart.

Kod (notebook laringizdagi bug tuzatishlar bilan)

# Oddiy scikit-learn gradient boosting (qo'shimcha install yo'q)
from sklearn.ensemble import GradientBoostingClassifier, HistGradientBoostingClassifier
gb = GradientBoostingClassifier(n_estimators=300, learning_rate=0.05, max_depth=3)

# XGBoost - zamonaviy API
from xgboost import XGBClassifier
xgb = XGBClassifier(n_estimators=500, learning_rate=0.05, max_depth=4,
                    eval_metric="logloss")   # use_label_encoder ni BERMANG (XGBoost 2.0 da olib tashlangan)
xgb.fit(X_train, y_train)

# LightGBM - early stopping endi CALLBACK larda (fit argument larida emas)
import lightgbm as lgb
m = lgb.LGBMClassifier(n_estimators=1000, learning_rate=0.05)
m.fit(X_tr, y_tr, eval_set=[(X_val, y_val)],
      callbacks=[lgb.early_stopping(50), lgb.log_evaluation(0)])
⚠️ Qochish kerak bo'lgan real bug lar (notebook review dan)
  • XGBoost: use_label_encoder 2.0 da olib tashlangan — uni berish xato chiqaradi. O'chiring.
  • LightGBM: fit() dagi early_stopping_rounds=/verbose= olib tashlangan — callbacks=[lgb.early_stopping(...), lgb.log_evaluation(0)] ishlating.
  • Leakage: hech qachon test set ni early-stopping eval_set sifatida ishlatmang — alohida validation split ishlating.

Qachon ishlatish + gotcha lar

✅ Kuchli tomonlari❌ Zaif tomonlari
Tabular data da odatda eng yaxshi aniqlikOverfit qilishi mumkin — early stopping va tuning kerak
Non-linearity, aralash turlar, missing value ni eplaydiRF dan ko'proq hyperparameter
Feature importance + SHAP tushuntirishlariKetma-ket → sekinroq train; kam tushunarli

Feature scaling kerak emas (tree-based). learning_rate + n_estimators ni early stopping bilan birga sozlang; bu yutuqning 80% i.

Intervyu savollari

SavolKuchli javob
Boosting nima?Ketma-ket ensemble, har bir model oldingilarning xatolarini tuzatadi; zaif o'rganuvchilar kuchliga birlashadi.
Bagging vs Boosting?Bagging = parallel, variance ni kamaytiradi (chuqur tree); Boosting = ketma-ket, bias ni kamaytiradi (sayoz tree).
AdaBoost vs Gradient Boosting?AdaBoost xato nuqtalarni qayta vaznlaydi; Gradient Boosting har yangi tree ni residual xatolarga (loss gradient i) moslaydi.
learning_rate nima qiladi?Har tree hissasini kichraytiradi; pastroq lr ko'proq tree talab qiladi, lekin yaxshiroq generalize qiladi.
Nega boosting overfit qiladi va qanday oldini olasiz?Tree qo'shish training xatoni kamaytiraveradi; early stopping, pastroq learning_rate, regularization, subsampling bilan oldini oling.

Keyingi bob: 14-bob — Model Evaluation va Metrics (confusion matrix, precision/recall, ROC-AUC, cross-validation — modellarni halol baholash).

14. Model Evaluation va Metrics

Model qurish — ishning yarmi; u haqiqatan yaxshi yoki yo'qligini bilish — ikkinchi yarmi. Bu bob — modellarni halol baholash uchun asboblar to'plami — va real ish uchun eng amaliy bob, to'g'ridan-to'g'ri Part 4 va Part 7 notebook laringizdan olingan.

Nega accuracy yetarli emas

⚠️ Accuracy paradoksi

Odamlarning 1% ini ta'sirlaydigan kasallikni tasavvur qiling. "Hech kim kasal emas" deydigan — doim class 0 bashorat qiladigan — model 99% accuracy beradi va butunlay foydasiz: u bironta bemorni ushlamaydi. Imbalanced data da accuracy yolg'on gapiradi. Sizga model qaysi turdagi xato qilishini ko'radigan metrikalar kerak.

Confusion matrix

Classification dagi hamma narsa shu yerdan boshlanadi. U bashoratlarni to'rtta katakka bo'ladi:

Bashorat 0Bashorat 1
Haqiqiy 0TN (true negative) ✅FP (false positive) ❌ "yolg'on signal"
Haqiqiy 1FN (false negative) ❌ "o'tkazib yubordi"TP (true positive) ✅
  • FP (Type I error) — positive deb bashorat qilindi, lekin emas (masalan, yaxshi email ni spam deb belgilash).
  • FN (Type II error) — haqiqiy positive o'tkazib yuborildi (masalan, kasal bemorga sog'lom dedi).

Precision / Recall / F1 (formulalar)

Confusion matrix danAccuracy = (TP + TN) / total umumiy to'g'rilik Precision = TP / (TP + FP) bashorat-positive lardan nechtasi to'g'ri edi Recall = TP / (TP + FN) haqiqiy-positive lardan nechtasini ushladik F1 = 2 × (Precision × Recall) / (Precision + Recall) harmonik o'rtacha
💡 Precision vs Recall — qaysi biriga ustuvorlik?
  • Recall — o'tkazib yuborish qimmat bo'lsa: rak skrining, fraud, kasallik — o'tkazib yuborilgan holatdan ko'ra yolg'on signal yaxshi.
  • Precision — yolg'on signal qimmat bo'lsa: spam filter (haqiqiy email ni o'chirmang), qimmat harakat tavsiya qilish.
  • F1 — ikkalasining muvozanati kerak bo'lsa (va class lar imbalanced).
🧩 Eslab qolish

Precision = "positive desam, to'g'ri aytdimmi?" Recall = "barcha positive larni topdimmi?" Mayda ko'zli to'r hamma narsani ushlaydi (yuqori recall), lekin axlatni ham (past precision); ehtiyotkor nayza yuqori precision ga ega, lekin ko'p baliqni o'tkazib yuboradi (past recall).

ROC-AUC, PR curve va threshold tuning

Classifier probability chiqaradi; 0.5 kesim — bu shunchaki default. Bu asboblar uni barcha threshold lar bo'ylab baholaydi:

  • ROC curve — threshold o'zgarganda True Positive Rate (recall) ni False Positive Rate ga qarshi chizadi. AUC (uning ostidagi maydon) = model tasodifiy positive ni tasodifiy negative dan yuqori joylashtirish ehtimoli. 1.0 = mukammal, 0.5 = tasodifiy.
  • Precision-Recall (PR) curve — imbalanced data uchun ROC dan yaxshiroq, chunki u oson true-negative larni e'tiborsiz qoldiradi.
  • Threshold tuning — recall ni oshirish uchun kesimni pasaytiring (masalan, 0.5 → 0.3) — precision evaziga ko'proq positive ushlaysiz. Cost kelishuvingizga mos threshold tanlash uchun predict_proba ishlating.

Cross-validation (bitta split ga ishonmang)

Bitta train/test split omadli yoki omadsiz bo'lishi mumkin. k-fold cross-validation data ni k qismga bo'lib, k−1 da o'rgatib, oxirgisida sinaydi, k marta aylanadi, keyin o'rtachalaydi — ancha ishonchli natija.

TuriQachon
K-FoldUmumiy regression
Stratified K-FoldClassification — har fold da class balansini saqlaydi (classifier lar uchun default)
TimeSeriesSplitVaqt data — hech qachon kelajakda o'rgatmang
LOOCVJuda kichik dataset (k = n; qimmat)
Nested CVHyperparameter sozlash paytida xolis natija
 
from sklearn.model_selection import cross_val_score
scores = cross_val_score(model, X_train, y_train, cv=5, scoring="f1")
print(scores.mean(), scores.std())   # fold lar bo'ylab o'rtacha ± tarqalish

Imbalanced data bilan ishlash

Bitta class kam bo'lsa (fraud, kasallik), bularni ishlating (Part 7 dan):

TexnikaQanday
To'g'ri metrikalarF1 / Recall / PR-AUC ishlating — hech qachon oddiy accuracy emas.
Class weightsclass_weight="balanced" — kam class dagi xatolarni ko'proq jazolash.
Minority ni oversampleKam-class qatorlarni nusxalash yoki sintez qilish (SMOTE, imbalanced-learn dan).
Majority ni undersampleKo'p-class qatorlarning bir qismini tashlash.
Threshold movingKam class ni ko'proq ushlash uchun 0.5 kesimni pasaytirish.
⚠️ Resample ni pipeline ichida, split dan keyin qiling

Faqat training fold larini oversample qiling — hech qachon test set ni emas, va hech qachon split dan oldin emas — aks holda nusxalangan qatorlar validation ga sizib o'tadi va soxta yuqori natija olasiz. SMOTE har fold da ishlashi uchun imblearn Pipeline ishlating.

Regression metrikalari (qisqacha)

MetrikaMa'nosi
MAEO'rtacha absolyut xato — o'rtacha farq, outlier larga chidamli
MSE / RMSEKvadrat xato — katta farqlarni jazolaydi; RMSE y birligida
R²Tushuntirilgan variance ulushi (1 = mukammal, 0 = o'rtachadan yaxshi emas)

Intervyu savollari

SavolKuchli javob
Nega accuracy yetarli emas?Imbalanced data da arzimas model yuqori accuracy beradi, lekin kam class dan bironta ushlamaydi — accuracy paradoksi.
Precision vs Recall?Precision = TP/(TP+FP) ("positive desam to'g'rimi"); Recall = TP/(TP+FN) ("barcha positive larni topdimmi").
Qachon recall? precision?O'tkazib yuborish qimmat bo'lsa recall (rak, fraud); yolg'on signal qimmat bo'lsa precision (spam).
F1 nima?Precision va recall ning harmonik o'rtachasi; ikkalasini muvozanatlaydi, imbalance uchun yaxshi.
ROC-AUC nimani anglatadi?Model tasodifiy positive ni tasodifiy negative dan yuqori joylashtirish ehtimoli; 1 = mukammal, 0.5 = tasodifiy.
ROC vs PR curve?Imbalanced data uchun PR curve ishlating — negative lar ko'p bo'lganda ROC optimistik ko'rinishi mumkin.
Nega cross-validation?Bitta split shovqinli; CV k split bo'ylab o'rtachalaydi. Classification uchun stratified ishlating.
Type I vs Type II error?Type I = false positive (FP); Type II = false negative (FN).
Imbalance ni qanday eplaysiz?To'g'ri metrikalar (F1/recall/PR-AUC), class weight, resampling (SMOTE), threshold moving.

Xulosa

MavzuEslab qolish kerak bo'lgan yagona narsa
Accuracy tuzog'iImbalanced data da foydasiz — xato turlariga qarang.
Confusion matrixTP/FP/FN/TN — barcha classification metrikalarining manbai.
Precision/Recall/F1Precision = positive da to'g'ri; Recall = barcha positive ushlandi; F1 = muvozanat.
ROC-AUC / PRThreshold lar bo'ylab baholash; imbalance uchun PR. Threshold ni sozlang.
Cross-validationk fold bo'ylab o'rtacha; classification uchun stratified.

Keyingi bob: 15-bob — Hyperparameter Tuning (GridSearch, RandomSearch, Optuna — eng yaxshi sozlamalarni topish).

15. Hyperparameter Tuning

Hozirgacha har bir algoritmda training dan oldin qo'yadigan tugmalar bor edi — k, C, max_depth, learning_rate. Tuning — bu eng yaxshi kombinatsiyani tizimli qidirish, cross-validation bilan baholangan. To'g'ri qilinsa, ko'pincha o'rtacha model bilan ajoyib model orasidagi farq shu.

Nima va nega

1-bobdan eslang: parameter larni fit() o'rganadi; hyperparameter larni siz tanlaysiz. Eng yaxshi hyperparameter lar uchun formula yo'q — siz qidirishingiz kerak: har bir nomzod uchun model o'rgatib, ularning cross-validation natijalarini taqqoslab.

⚠️ CV bilan baholang, hech qachon test set bilan emas

Tuning — ko'p sozlamani sinab, eng yaxshisini saqlash demak — agar ularni test set da baholasangiz, yashirincha unga moslaysiz. Training data da cross-validation bilan sozlang, test set ga esa faqat eng oxirida bir marta tegining.

Grid Search

GridSearchCV siz belgilagan grid dagi har bir kombinatsiyani sinab, eng yaxshisini saqlaydi (CV natijasi bo'yicha). To'liq va grid ichida eng yaxshisini topishi kafolatlangan — lekin u kombinatorik portlaydi.

⚠️ Kombinatorik portlash

3 qiymat × 4 qiymat × 5 qiymat = 60 kombinatsiya, × 5-fold CV = 300 model fit. Yana bitta hyperparameter qo'shsangiz, u ko'payadi. Grid search faqat bir nechta hyperparameter va kichik grid lar uchun amaliy.

Random Search

RandomizedSearchCV siz bergan oraliqlardan belgilangan sondagi tasodifiy kombinatsiyalarni tanlaydi. Byudjetni n_iter bilan boshqarasiz.

ℹ️ Nega random ko'pincha grid dan yaxshi

Odatda faqat bir-ikkita hyperparameter haqiqatan muhim. Grid o'zining ko'p fit ini muhim larining belgilangan qiymatlarida muhim bo'lmaganlarini o'zgartirishga isrof qiladi. Random search har birining ko'proq farqli qiymatlarini o'rganadi, shuning uchun bir xil byudjet bilan odatda yaxshiroq model topadi — ayniqsa ko'p hyperparameter bo'lganda.

Optuna (Bayesian optimization)

Grid va Random "xabarsiz" — har bir trial oldingi trial lar nimani o'rganganini e'tiborsiz qoldiradi. Optuna aqlliroq: u keyingi qayerga qarashni hal qilish uchun o'tgan natijalardan foydalanadi (Bayesian optimization / TPE), istiqbolli hududlarga yo'naladi.

  • Study = butun optimization; Trial = bitta nomzod; Objective = maksimallashtirish/minimallashtirish uchun CV natijasini qaytaradigan funksiya.
  • Pruning = umidsiz trial larni erta to'xtatish (aniq yomon config ni o'rgatishga vaqt isrof qilmaslik).
  • Orders of magnitude qamrab oladigan oraliqlar uchun (learning_rate, C, alpha) suggest_float(..., log=True) ishlating.
import optuna
def objective(trial):
    params = {
        "max_depth": trial.suggest_int("max_depth", 3, 10),
        "learning_rate": trial.suggest_float("learning_rate", 1e-3, 0.3, log=True),
    }
    model = XGBClassifier(**params)
    return cross_val_score(model, X_train, y_train, cv=5, scoring="f1").mean()

study = optuna.create_study(direction="maximize")
study.optimize(objective, n_trials=100)
print(study.best_params)

Taqqoslash

MetodQanday qidiradiNima uchun mos
Grid SearchHar bir kombinatsiya (to'liq)Kam hyperparameter, kichik grid lar
Random SearchTasodifiy sample lar (belgilangan byudjet)Ko'p hyperparameter; tez kuchli baseline
Optuna (Bayesian)O'tgan trial lardan o'rganadi + pruningQimmat modellar; eng yaxshisini siqib chiqarish (boosting, NN)

Eng yaxshi amaliyot (va leakage yo'q)

from sklearn.model_selection import RandomizedSearchCV
from sklearn.pipeline import Pipeline

# Preprocessing ni pipeline ICHIGA qo'ying - tuning uni har CV fold da qayta fit qiladi (leakage yo'q)
pipe = Pipeline([("scaler", StandardScaler()), ("clf", SVC())])
params = {"clf__C": [0.1, 1, 10, 100], "clf__gamma": ["scale", 0.01, 0.1]}

search = RandomizedSearchCV(pipe, params, n_iter=20, cv=5, scoring="f1", n_jobs=-1)
search.fit(X_train, y_train)
print(search.best_params_, search.best_score_)
best_model = search.best_estimator_   # allaqachon butun training data da qayta fit qilingan
  • Pipeline ichida sozlang — shunda scaling/imputing har fold ning training qismida qayta fit bo'ladi (2-bobdagi leakage ning oldini oladi).
  • Log scale ishlating learning_rate, C, alpha uchun (ular orders of magnitude qamrab oladi).
  • Qo'pol dan nozikka — avval keng oraliqlar, keyin yaxshi hududga yaqinlashing.
  • Coarse-to-fine byudjet: kam epoch bilan ko'p trial — ko'p epoch bilan kam trial dan yaxshi.

Intervyu savollari

SavolKuchli javob
Grid vs Random search?Grid har bir kombinatsiyani sinaydi (to'liq, portlaydi); Random belgilangan byudjetni sample qiladi va ko'p hyperparameter bo'lganda odatda yaxshiroq model topadi.
Nega random search ko'pincha yaxshi?Faqat bir nechta hyperparameter muhim; random ularning ko'proq farqli qiymatlarini bir xil byudjet bilan o'rganadi.
Optuna nima qo'shadi?Bayesian optimization — u istiqbolli keyingilarni tanlash uchun o'tgan trial lardan foydalanadi, plus yomon trial larni pruning.
Tuning paytida leakage ni qanday oldini olasiz?Preprocessing ni Pipeline ga qo'ying va cross-validation bilan baholang; hech qachon test set da sozlamang.
Nega tuning paytida CV?Bitta split hyperparameter larni o'sha split ga overfit qiladi; CV ishonchli natija beradi.
Nested CV nima uchun?Hyperparameter ham sozlaganda xolis natija bahosi uchun (ichki loop sozlaydi, tashqi loop baholaydi).
Nega learning_rate/C uchun log scale?Ular orders of magnitude bo'ylab muhim; log sampling ularni teng qamrab oladi.

Xulosa

MavzuEslab qolish kerak bo'lgan yagona narsa
NimaEng yaxshi hyperparameter larni qidirish, cross-validation bilan baholangan.
GridHar bir kombinatsiya — to'liq, lekin portlaydi.
RandomTasodifiy byudjet — ko'p hyperparameter bilan odatda yaxshiroq.
OptunaBayesian + pruning — qimmat modellar uchun eng samarali.
Leakage yo'qPipeline ichida, CV bilan sozlang, hech qachon test set da emas.

Keyingi bob: 16-bob — Unsupervised Learning (PCA va clustering — label siz tuzilmani topish).

16. Unsupervised Learning (PCA va Clustering)

Hozirgacha har bir bob supervised edi — o'rganish uchun label (y) bor edi. Unsupervised learning da label yo'q: faqat X. Maqsad — yashirin tuzilmani o'zi topish. Ikki asosiy vazifa: dimensionality reduction (PCA) va clustering (guruhlash).

Unsupervised learning nima?

🧩 O'xshatish

Supervised learning — javoblar kaliti bilan o'qish. Unsupervised — bir uyum label siz rasm berilib, ularni guruhlarga ajratish so'ralgani — toifalarni oldindan bilmaysiz, ularni topasiz. Tekshiradigan "to'g'ri javob" yo'q, shuning uchun uni baholash qiyinroq.

VazifaMaqsadMisollar
Dimensionality reductionKamroq feature, ma'lumotni saqlashPCA, t-SNE — siqish, vizualizatsiya, tezlik
ClusteringO'xshash nuqtalarni guruhlashK-Means, DBSCAN — customer segment, anomaliya aniqlash

PCA (Principal Component Analysis)

PCA ko'p feature ni bir nechta principal component ga kamaytiradi — data dagi eng ko'p variance (tarqalish) ni ushlaydigan yangi o'qlar — iloji boricha kam ma'lumot yo'qotib.

🧩 O'xshatish

3D obyekt 2D soya tashlaydi. PCA soyasi eng ko'p tafsilotni saqlaydigan burchakni topadi — o'lchamlarni siqib, shaklni iloji boricha saqlaydi.

  • Nega ishlatish: training ni tezlashtirish, curse of dimensionality (8-bob) ga qarshi, shovqinni olib tashlash va yuqori o'lchamli data ni 2D da vizualizatsiya qilish.
  • explained_variance_ratio_ har bir component qancha ma'lumot saqlaganini aytadi (masalan, "2 component variance ning 95% ini saqlaydi").
  • Avval scale qiling! PCA variance ga asoslangan, shuning uchun scale qilinmagan feature lar bosib ketadi — PCA dan oldin doim StandardScaler.
  • t-SNE / UMAP — faqat 2D vizualizatsiya uchun non-linear metodlar (cluster larni ko'rish uchun ajoyib, lekin chiqishini model feature sifatida ishlatmang).

K-Means clustering + elbow metodi

K-Means data ni k ta cluster ga bo'ladi. Siz k ni tanlaysiz; u cluster markazlarini topadi.

K-Means loop1) k ta tasodifiy centroid tanlash 2) Har nuqtani eng yaqin centroid ga biriktirish 3) Har centroid ni o'z nuqtalarining o'rtachasiga ko'chirish 4) Centroid lar to'xtaguncha 2-3 ni takrorlash
💡 k ni tanlash — elbow metodi

K-Means dan k ni oldindan tanlash kerak. Inertia (cluster ichidagi umumiy masofa) ni k ga qarshi chizing: k oshgani sayin u doim pasayadi, lekin biror nuqtada foyda tekislanadi — o'sha "tirsak (elbow)" yaxshi k. Yoki silhouette score ishlating. Yana: K-Means masofaga asoslangan, shuning uchun avval feature larni scale qiling, va u taxminan sharsimon, teng o'lchamli cluster larni faraz qiladi.

DBSCAN va Hierarchical clustering

MetodG'oyaKuchli tomoni
K-Meansk centroid, masofani minimallashtirishTez; k kerak; faqat sharsimon cluster lar
